Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the magnet framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 677. The resulting operator achieves bound O(733/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to magnet structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 677/733 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The magnet operator L associated with invariance principle theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 86. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 28 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.
